Real CI [38393187129](https://github.com/yigini/soilfer-lims/actions/runs/38393187129), exact head `9ae02f6ab7651d2ca887a98722f6a20ace3cc471`, completed FAILURE at 12:31:44 UTC. The server test process reported 350 unique passing suites and no FAIL suite header, then exhausted its existing 8 GB CI heap and exited 134. This is incomplete coverage, not a full PASS. Candidate/baseline Docker builds, real boundary rehearsal and readiness acceptance were SKIPPED. No retry, replacement CI, ready-for-audit notice, final native handoff, merge or production operation has been dispatched.

The closed original log archive, extracted server log, run/jobs/PR API records and per-file hashes are retained in `WP/backlog-resume-20261013/ci-38393187129-completed-failure/index.json`. Retainer `retain-ci38393187129-completed-failure.py` ran once and must never be replayed or overwrite this boundary. The earlier local 362-suite / 5,508-test coverage at `c56414a` remains its separate, honestly combined result. Application head and all previous raw reports remain unchanged.

Investigation: `npm test` forces Jest `--runInBand`, keeping the complete suite in one process. The installed Jest scheduler explicitly permits one worker when `workerIdleMemoryLimit` is set, and its worker can recycle between suites. A proposed test-only correction uses one serial worker with a 512 MB idle recycle threshold, preserving discovery, ordinary global/per-suite database setup, assertions and failure propagation. No heap increase or production change is proposed. Scope is raised with Claudio before changing the PR head; green complete CI and a new exact-head audit are still required. Fresh actual native read confirms the correct thread, both empty composers and the unanswered human #103 clarification; human cards/drafts remain untouched.
